│ Response    │ - `attn_tp_size = tp_size // dp_size` (the TP size within each D
│             │ - `attn_tp_rank = tp_rank % attn_tp_size` (the rank within the D
│             │ - `attn_dp_rank = tp_rank // attn_tp_size` (which DP worker this
│             │
│             │ So the `tp_cpu_group` contains all TP ranks across all DP worker
│             │ with this group, we're gathering from all ranks.

│             │ │ 'file_text': '# DP Attention Scheduling        │
│             │ │ Optimization\n\n## Overview\nThis optimization │
│             │ │ reduces communication overhead in DP (Data     │
│             │ │ Parallel) attention scheduling for speculative │
│             │ │ decoding by only gathering data from the first │
│             │ │ TP (Tensor Parallel) rank of each DP worker,   │
│             │ │ rather than all TP ranks.\n\n## Problem\nThe   │
│             │ │ original implementation used                   │
│             │ │ `all_gather_into_tensor` to gather data from   │
│             │ │ all `dp_size * attn_tp_size` ranks, but only   │
│             │ │ used the data from the first TP rank of each   │
│             │ │ DP worker (`[:, 0, ...]`). This resulted       │
│             │ │ in:\n- Unnecessary memory allocation for       │
│             │ │ communication buffers\n- Excessive network     │
│             │ │ bandwidth usage\n- Higher CPU overhead for     │
│             │ │ tensor preparation\n\n## Solution\nCreated a   │
│             │ │ dedicated communication group                  │
│             │ │ (`dp_attn_coordination_cpu_group`) containing  │
│             │ │ only the first TP rank of each DP worker. This │
│             │ │ group is used for DP attention coordination,   │
│             │ │ reducing communication overhead by a factor of │
│             │ │ `attn_tp_size`.\n\n## Changes Made\n\n### 1.   │
│             │ │ `python/sglang/srt/managers/scheduler.py`\n\n… │
│             │ │ Added group initialization (lines              │
│             │ │ 365-376):\n```python\n# Create a group         │
│             │ │ containing only the first TP rank of each DP   │
│             │ │ worker\n# This is used for efficient DP        │
│             │ │ attention                                      │
│             │ │ coordination\nself.dp_attn_coordination_cpu_g… │
│             │ │ = None\nif                                     │
│             │ │ self.server_args.enable_dp_attention and       │
│             │ │ self.attn_tp_size > 1:\n    # Ranks that are   │
│             │ │ the first TP rank in each DP worker\n          │
│             │ │ dp_attn_coordination_ranks = [\n        i *    │
│             │ │ self.attn_tp_size for i in                     │
│             │ │ range(self.dp_size)\n    ]\n                   │
│             │ │ self.dp_attn_coordination_cpu_group =          │
│             │ │ torch.distributed.new_group(\n                 │
│             │ │ ranks=dp_attn_coordination_ranks,\n            │
│             │ │ backend="gloo",\n    )\n```\n\n#### Optimized  │
│             │ │ `coordinate_spec_dp_attn_batch` (lines         │
│             │ │ 1415-1469):\n- Only the first TP rank          │
│             │ │ (`attn_tp_rank == 0`) participates in the      │
│             │ │ all_gather\n- Uses reduced tensor size:        │
│             │ │ `(dp_size, 1)` instead of `(dp_size,           │
│             │ │ attn_tp_size, 1)`\n- Non-first TP ranks get    │
│             │ │ the result via broadcast\n- **Communication    │
│             │ │ reduction: `attn_tp_size`x less data           │
│             │ │ transferred**\n\n#### Optimized                │
│             │ │ `prepare_mlp_sync_batch_raw` (lines            │
│             │ │ 1843-1997):\n- Added parameters:               │
│             │ │ `attn_tp_rank`,                                │
│             │ │ `dp_attn_coordination_cpu_group`\n- Only the   │
│             │ │ first TP rank participates in the              │
│             │ │ all_gather\n- Uses reduced tensor size:        │
│             │ │ `(dp_size, 6)` instead of `(dp_size,           │
│             │ │ attn_tp_size, 6)`\n- Results are broadcast to  │
│             │ │ other TP ranks\n- **Communication reduction:   │
│             │ │ `attn_tp_size`x less data transferred**\n\n### │
│             │ │ 2. `python/sglang/bench_one_batch.py`\n\n####  │
│             │ │ Updated `_maybe_prepare_mlp_sync_batch` (lines │
│             │ │ 268-285):\n- Added `attn_tp_rank=0`            │
│             │ │ parameter\n- Added                             │
│             │ │ `dp_attn_coordination_cpu_group=None`          │
│             │ │ parameter\n- Added missing parameters:         │
│             │ │ `enable_two_batch_overlap`,                    │
│             │ │ `enable_deepep_moe`, `deepep_mode`\n- Added    │
│             │ │ import for `DeepEPMode`\n\n## Performance      │
│             │ │ Impact\n\n### Communication Reduction\n|       │
│             │ │ Configuration | Original Elements | Optimized  │
│             │ │ Elements | Reduction                           │
│             │ │ |\n|--------------|-------------------|------… │
│             │ │ DP=4, ATTN_TP=2 | 8 | 4 | 50% |\n| DP=8,       │
│             │ │ ATTN_TP=4 | 32 | 8 | 75% |\n| DP=16, ATTN_TP=8 │
│             │ │ | 128 | 16 | 87.5% |\n| DP=32, ATTN_TP=16 |    │
│             │ │ 512 | 32 | 93.8% |\n\n### Benefits\n1.         │
│             │ │ **Reduced memory allocation**: Smaller         │
│             │ │ communication buffers\n2. **Lower network      │
│             │ │ bandwidth**: Less data transferred across the  │
│             │ │ network\n3. **Faster all_gather operations**:  │
│             │ │ Fewer elements to gather\n4. **Reduced CPU     │
│             │ │ overhead**: Less tensor preparation work\n5.   │
│             │ │ **Better scalability**: More efficient for     │
│             │ │ large DP configurations\n\n## Correctness\nThe │
│             │ │ optimization maintains functional              │
│             │ │ equivalence:\n- The first TP rank of each DP   │
│             │ │ worker still participates in coordination\n-   │
│             │ │ The result is properly synchronized to all TP  │
│             │ │ ranks via broadcast\n- Fallback to original    │
│             │ │ behavior when the dedicated group is not       │
│             │ │ created\n- All existing tests should pass      │
│             │ │ without modification\n\n## Testing\n- Syntax   │

│             │ │ Completed\n\n## Notes\n- The optimization only │
│             │ │ applies when `enable_dp_attention=True` and    │
│             │ │ `attn_tp_size > 1`\n- When `attn_tp_size ==    │
│             │ │ 1`, the original behavior is maintained\n- The │
│             │ │ optimization is most beneficial for            │
│             │ │ speculative decoding workloads\n'}             │
